play_btn
precision mediump float;
varying vec2 v_uv;
uniform sampler2D u_tex;
uniform float u_clipBot;
uniform float u_clipTop;
uniform float u_fade;
void main()
    vec4 color = texture2D(u_tex, v_uv);
    float y = gl_FragCoord.y;
    float fade = max(u_fade, 1.0);
    float aBot = smoothstep(u_clipBot, u_clipBot + fade, y);
    float aTop = 1.0 - smoothstep(u_clipTop - fade, u_clipTop, y);
    gl_FragColor = color * min(aBot, aTop);
